# Skills · B · part 56

955 entries, brainstorming to branch-diff-gsheet. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **brainstorming**: Use before creative work whose requirements or design are not yet agreed — new features, new components, behavior changes with open questions. Skip when an approved spec or ruling already fixes the design. Explores user intent, requirements and design before implementation. - **brainstorming**: Use when something needs to be designed or built and the shape of the solution is still open — before any code. Triggers on: "어떻게 만들지?", "기능 설계해줘", "아키텍처 잡아줘", "옵션 더 뽑아줘", "발산해줘", "design this feature", "how should we build X?". - **brainstorming**: Use when deciding what to build: a new feature, component or subsystem, a vague or conflicted request, a requirement that spans several capabilities, or a product-level design choice with real tradeoffs. Produces intent, constraints, capability map, spec and observable acceptance. Not for service or module structure (architecture-simplicity), consequential tool selection (production-first-decisions), executing already-specified multi-slice work (writing-plans), or non-code judgment work (fable-method). ([oleg494/coding-kit](https://github.com/oleg494/coding-kit/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/brainstorming) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **brainstorming**: Resolve design decisions before implementation when requirements, contracts, or product trade-offs need clarification; scale the process to existing decisions and task risk. ([OutlitAI/skills](https://github.com/OutlitAI/skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/brainstorming) · ★3)
- **brainstorming**: Use before any creative, strategic, or analytical work — developing legal strategies, planning a business or a go-to-market move, shaping a product or feature, designing a technical approach, or solving open-ended problems. Those domains are detected out of the box; a profile may add others (content, teaching, infrastructure). Turns rough ideas into actionable plans through dialogue, with five purposes — define a task, plan a build (its order, who runs each step, what each hands on), decide between options, design a system, or run a quick check — and adapts its depth to the question's scope,… ([rohankgeorge/thinking-tools](https://github.com/rohankgeorge/thinking-tools/tree/HEAD/plugins/brainstorming/skills/brainstorming) · ★3)
- **brainstorming**: Find out what the user actually wants before building something new. Runs when the user asks for it, or when a request asks for something new and leaves out a decision that would change the result; skips when the request is already complete. Sizes the work out loud, plays understanding back with guesses marked, states every default, offers throwaway spikes, splits multi-project requests, checks a written design with a fresh reader, and builds only after "proceed". Question rounds use the grill-me skill. ([ruskicoder/system-prompts](https://github.com/ruskicoder/system-prompts/tree/HEAD/prompt-orchestrator/.agents/skills/brainstorming) · ★3 · 4 copies)

([vietlinhh02/cogninote](https://github.com/vietlinhh02/cogninote/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/methodology/brainstorming) · 2 copies)
- **brainstorming**: 需求澄清与方案批准 Skill：命中真实需求未决或用户明确要求时，逐项收敛目标、用户价值、非目标、约束、成功标准和方案取舍，并取得用户明确批准。 触发条件（满足任一）： 1. 用户希望澄清新功能或行为变更的真实需求（如“先把需求想清楚”“我们到底要解决什么问题”） 2. 请求中的目标、价值、边界或成功标准会实质影响决策但尚不明确（例如“做一个企业级登录，直接开工”） 3. 用户希望比较决策级方案并批准方向（如“给我几个选择并推荐一个”“这个功能还值得继续吗”） 不触发（留给 spec-driven-workflow、openspec、diagnosing-bugs、tdd 或对应执行 Skill）： - 已有可定位的 Brainstorming 批准记录，只要求写规格、技术设计或任务，留给 openspec - 目标明确的故障诊断、已有批准规格的实现/测试，或明确、机械、低风险且不改变用户可观察行为的小改动 默认路由：存在真实需求未决或用户明确要求澄清时进入本 Skill；需求决策已明确且有可定位证据时可直接交给 OpenSpec。只有经仓库证据确认明确、机械、低风险、易回滚且不改变用户可观察行为、公共契约、数据、权限或架构时才走快速路径。 ([Vitus213/specpatrol](https://github.com/Vitus213/specpatrol/tree/HEAD/src/specpatrol/skillpack/skills/brainstorming))
- **brainstorming**: Use when a user brings a new feature, design question, or architectural decision that needs exploration before code. Guides Socratic discovery of requirements, proposes multiple approaches, and produces an approved spec under docs/ before any implementation begins. ([vonschau/symbiopowers](https://github.com/vonschau/symbiopowers/tree/HEAD/skills/brainstorming))
- **brainstorming**: Explore intent, requirements, and design options before committing to a plan, surfacing unknowns and trade-offs early. Use when the Router routes an ambiguous or STANDARD/EPIC request at its start, before the planning skill. ([Vtquan123/strix](https://github.com/Vtquan123/strix/tree/HEAD/skills/brainstorming))
- **brainstorming**: 在任何创意工作之前必须使用 - 创建功能、构建组件、添加功能或修改行为。在实施之前探索用户意图、需求和设计。合并了 discuss-requirements 和 describe-interaction 的功能。 ([walker-hzx/AI-Assistant](https://github.com/walker-hzx/AI-Assistant/tree/HEAD/skills/brainstorming))
- **brainstorming**: Use before any creative work — creating features, building components, adding functionality, or modifying behavior — OR when revising an existing feature design. Triggers on: 'create feature X', 'build X', 'add functionality', 'update feature X', 'revise the design', 'change the plan for Y', 'modify the existing design'. Explores intent and design (new features) or focused change delta (revisions) before implementation. - **brainstorming**: Mandatory first step before building anything new or changing behavior. Use this to scope a feature you've decided to build; to validate whether an idea is worth building at all (YC-style forcing questions), use gstack-office-hours first.
